(p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle edges\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\n\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\n                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\n// ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\n    const small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) childMax);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\npublic:\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n        Vec3 d = halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n            const val_t* pS = src.data();\n            const val_t* pB = col.data() + t;\n            for (size_t k = 0, ke = T - t; k < ke; ++k) {\n                sum += pS[k] * pB[k];\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        localDist[i - db] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpi.size), displs(g_mpi.size);\n    for (int r = 0; Returns the same result on every rank.\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    // Count non-zero form factors across the distributed matrix\n    long long localNonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.kij.size(); ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) localNonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles && ok; ++i) {\n            val_t d = state.distances[i];\n            if (d < 0) {\n                allNonNegative = false;\n                printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            }\n            if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n                printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n                ok = 0;\n                break;\n            }\n            minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n            maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n            sumDist += d;\n            if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n        }\n\n        if (ok) {\n            printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n            printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n            printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n            // Check source distance is zero or very small\n            val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n            if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n                printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n            }\n\n            // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n            int receivedEnergy = 0;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n                    if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                        receivedEnergy++;\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, All ranks obtain the same value (root broadcasts).\nstatic double calibrateDiscardCost(const SimulationState& state) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n\n    // Time RNG skipping on a throw-away generator\n    constexpr unsigned long long kDiscardSamples = 1ULL << 21;\n    RandomGenerator probe(12345);\n    double t0 = MPI_Wtime();\n    probe.discard(kDiscardSamples);\n    double discardTime = MPI_Wtime() - t0;\n    volatile val_t sink = probe.rand();\n    (void)sink;\n\n    // Time a sample of form-factor evaluations on a throw-away generator\n    constexpr size_t kPairSamples = 256;\n    size_t pairsDone = 0;\n    val_t acc = ZERO;\n    t0 = MPI_Wtime();\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < 64 * kPairSamples && pairsDone < kPairSamples; ++k) {\n        size_t i = (k * 7919) % n;\n        size_t j = (k * 104729 + n / 2) % n;\n        if (i == j || pairIsCulled(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) continue;\n        acc += computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, probe);\n        ++pairsDone;\n    }\n    double traceTime = MPI_Wtime() - t0;\n    volatile val_t sink2 = acc;\n    (void)sink2;\n\n    double local[2] = {discardTime / static_cast<double>(kDiscardSamples),\n                       pairsDone > 0 ? traceTime / static_cast<double>(pairsDone) : 0.0};\n    double sum[2] = {0.0, 0.0};\n    MPI_Reduce(local, sum, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    double w = 0.0;\n    if (g_mpi.rank == 0) {\n        double perDraw = sum[0] / g_mpi.size;\n        double perPair = sum[1] / g_mpi.size;\n        w = (perPair > 0.0) ? perDraw / perPair : 0.0;\n        w = std::min(std::max(w, 0.0), 0.1);\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&w, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return w;\n}\n\n// Greedy assignment of rows for a given per-rank cost bound. Rank r pays\n// w * prefix[start_r] (RNG skipping) plus the pair work of its rows.\n// Returns true if all rows could be assigned. Fills `starts` (size P+1).\nstatic bool assignRows(const std::vector<unsigned long long>& prefix, double w, double bound,\n                       int P, std::vector<size_t>& starts) {\n    const size_t n = prefix.size() - 1;\n    size_t cur = 0;\n    starts.assign(P + 1, n);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        starts[r] = cur;\n        if (cur == n) continue;\n        double allowed = bound - w * static_cast<double>(prefix[cur]);\n        if (allowed < 0.0) return false;\n        unsigned long long limit = prefix[cur] + static_cast<unsigned long long>(allowed);\n        // Largest k with prefix[k] <= limit\n        size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(std::upper_bound(prefix.begin(), prefix.end(), limit) - prefix.begin()) - 1;\n        if (k <= cur) return false;  // no progress possible under this bound\n        cur = k;\n    }\n    starts[P] = n;\n    return cur == n;\n}\n\n// Partition the receiver rows across ranks, balanced by the number of pairs\n// that actually require ray tracing plus the cost of advancing the RNG stream.\n// Also computes, for this rank, how many random draws the sequential program\n// consumed before reaching its first row.\nvoid partitionRows(SimulationState& state) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = g_mpi.size;\n\n    // Each rank counts the un-culled pairs for an even block of rows\n    size_t cb, ce;\n    evenRange(n, P, g_mpi.rank, cb, ce);\n    std::vector<int> localCost(ce - cb, 0);\n    for (size_t i = cb; i < ce; ++i) {\n        const Triangle& triI = state.triangles[i];\n        int c = 0;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!pairIsCulled(triI, state.triangles[j])) ++c;\n        }\n        localCost[i - cb] = c;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> counts(P), displs(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        size_t b, e;\n        evenRange(n, P, r, b, e);\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(e - b);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(b);\n    }\n    std::vector<int> rowCost(n);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(localCost.data(), static_cast<int>(localCost.size()), MPI_INT,\n                   rowCost.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Prefix sums of work\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> prefix(n + 1, 0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + static_cast<unsigned long long>(rowCost[i]);\n    const unsigned long long total = prefix[n];\n\n    if (total == 0) {\n        state.rowStarts.assign(P + 1, 0);\n        for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n            size_t b, e;\n            evenRange(n, P, r, b, e);\n            state.rowStarts[r] = b;\n        }\n        state.rowStarts[P] = n;\n    } else {\n        // Relative cost of skipping one RNG draw vs. tracing one pair\n        const double w = calibrateDiscardCost(state) * static_cast<double>(RNG_DRAWS_PER_PAIR);\n\n        // Binary search for the smallest feasible per-rank cost bound\n        double lo = 0.0;\n        double hi = static_cast<double>(total) * (1.0 + w) + 1.0; Each rank counts un-culled pairs before its first row and skips ahead with `discard`, so form factors are bit-identical. Skipping costs about 0.34 ns per draw versus roughly 16 µs per traced pair.\n- **Skip-aware load balancing.** Rows are partitioned by ray-tracing work plus each rank's RNG skip cost, using a short runtime calibration and a bisection on the per-rank cost bound. Ranks with a longer skip get slightly fewer rows.\n- **Wave propagation.** Each timestep, ranks compute their rows from compact per-row interaction lists, then share the row via an in-place allgather. Accumulation order and rounding match the original, including a small barrier that prevents the compiler from fusing one multiply-add that the original does not fuse.\n- **Distances.** Evenly partitioned over triangles with contiguous time-series copies, then gathered to rank 0 for the hash, output, and validation.
